Ppan_dim_oniumJan 25 2012, 09:54 PM
+2 for Sonic. Way to finish out the Blitz!+2 for shadowmanwkp. Phew. That one took a while.I'm pretty focused on fixing my uploads, so no new Blitz! for a bit, but I have posted two new screenshots for you guys to take a stab at.RULES: 1. Obey the forum's posting rules.2. One game title answer per person per question. If you get something wrong and I correct you, you are allowed to guess again. Once for each time I correct you.3. You can answer both of the non-Blitz! questions in one post. You can answer only two Blitz! questions per day. That makes a total of 4 questions and 8 points possible. If there are Extra Questions posted, you can get even more points by answering as many of those as you'd like.4. Each Blitz! has it's own rules, but I want to reiterate some of the common rules here. Two Blitz! questions, per person, per day. If you are corrected, you may guess again within that day, but only at the two you had initially guessed at. If you pick a question as one of your two that only had the system guess open, it still counts as one of your two questions despite it only being worth 1 point.5. If a question goes unanswered for a certain amount of time or guesses (at my discretion) I will post a new question with the old one in an EXTRA QUESTIONS section.6. Titles are worth one point, a correct system guess is worth 1 bonus point. Extra Questions vary.7. If you change your display name, PM me or include a note in your post along with your next guess and I'll update the scoreboard.8. Games may be repeated.9. As I live in North America, the screens will be of a North American release unless it is unavailable in North America or unless otherwise specified.10. Spelling is at my discretion. If I feel it is obvious that what you typed was intended to be what the correct answer is, points will be rewarded. If I have no idea if it was even in English, then you need to take a typing class.11. I do not include re-releases on WiiWare (Virtual Console), PSN, Steam, etc. as a separate console. For example, Final Fantasy: Mystic Quest was re-released as a download on Wii, but I will not post the Wii version.12. I will do my best to not post full reboots/remakes, such as Final Fantasy 3 on DS, without noting that it is a reboot/remake.13. Arcade is a "console". I also do not count PC/Mac/Linux as separate consoles.14. DS/3DS games may include both screens or just one of the screens at my discretion and without warning.15. When answering, you must specify which question you are answering with its number. For Example: Q1: The answer is Super Mario Bros. for NES.16. I am not infallible. If I mess something up, PM me and I'll fix it.INCENTIVE/PRIZES: FREE STUFF!Since I am not a forum official, I cannot offer GPs. There are other incentives though. Each post ups your post count and gets you some GPs plus it can be fun to test your gaming knowledge and see your name move up the scoreboard. Here's what I can offer though:1. Nintendo DS ROMs *2. Humble Bundle gift urls **If you get 5 points, PM me and I can send you whichever you like.* Last I checked I have 280+ NDS ROMs. Here's a link to the text file with a list of all my NDS ROMs. It's updated whenever I grab a new one. Feel free to pick one out every 5 points and PM me. That way, you won't have to spend your DPs and use up the site's bandwidth downloading it.http://dl.dropbox.co...0Downloaded.txt** I also have some Humble Bundle gift urls for almost all of the bundles so far. Though I have posted the games for free, the urls will allow you to gain access to keys to get the games on Steam, OnLive, Direct2Drive, and/or Desura. These keys can no longer be acquired as these packs are now closed, so when you get 5 points, feel free to PM me with a request.PRIZES GIVEN:2 NDS ROMs1 Bundle urlSCOREBOARD:tedsb16 - 101Channel 28 - 83Galdion the wise - 72OnceUponATime13 - 54gaga blues - 47The Lone Wanderer - 21shadowmanwkp - 21Sleightblind - 13Bash the Government - 12fartbomb - 12polopbob - 9TheGimp - 8bhudachong - 7SP_ - 6Sonic - 6Zero v2.0 - 5kardo - 4Calvin Luther - 3MFZBdude - 3sylfi - 3AsrarQ - 2monomer - 2Ragnell vs. Alondite - 2WinterNut - 2Achul Ma - 1c.cooper_06 - 1Josh Loves Anarchy - 1kalmis - 1KillerEwok - 1EXTRA QUESTIONS: These are questions that either went unanswered for too long, or were guessed incorrectly too many times.2 points for a title, 2 points for the system when accompanying a correct title. 4 points possible.EQ1: http://i1186.photobu...creens/Q186.jpgHINT1: Subtitle is a bad reflection.HINT2: Main title is a four letter male name.EQ2: http://i1186.photobu...creens/Q214.jpgHINT: Animal Place + shapeQUESTIONS:Q173: http://i1186.photobucket.com/albums/z365/pan_dim_onium/Game%20Screens/Q210.jpgQ174: http://i1186.photobucket.com/albums/z365/pan_dim_onium/Game%20Screens/Q205.jpg

+2 for Channel28.I was able to put together a new Blitz! Though it is rather generic lol. Same rules apply, 2 guesses, per person, per day in addition to the regular questions. Please do peruse the special rules.RULES: 1. Obey the forum's posting rules.2. One game title answer per person per question. If you get something wrong and I correct you, you are allowed to guess again. Once for each time I correct you.3. You can answer both of the non-Blitz! questions in one post. You can answer only two Blitz! questions per day. That makes a total of 4 questions and 8 points possible. If there are Extra Questions posted, you can get even more points by answering as many of those as you'd like.4. Each Blitz! has it's own rules, but I want to reiterate some of the common rules here. Two Blitz! questions, per person, per day. If you are corrected, you may guess again within that day, but only at the two you had initially guessed at. If you pick a question as one of your two that only had the system guess open, it still counts as one of your two questions despite it only being worth 1 point.5. If a question goes unanswered for a certain amount of time or guesses (at my discretion) I will post a new question with the old one in an EXTRA QUESTIONS section.6. Titles are worth one point, a correct system guess is worth 1 bonus point. Extra Questions vary.7. If you change your display name, PM me or include a note in your post along with your next guess and I'll update the scoreboard.8. Games may be repeated.9. As I live in North America, the screens will be of a North American release unless it is unavailable in North America or unless otherwise specified.10. Spelling is at my discretion. If I feel it is obvious that what you typed was intended to be what the correct answer is, points will be rewarded. If I have no idea if it was even in English, then you need to take a typing class.11. I do not include re-releases on WiiWare (Virtual Console), PSN, Steam, etc. as a separate console. For example, Final Fantasy: Mystic Quest was re-released as a download on Wii, but I will not post the Wii version.12. I will do my best to not post full reboots/remakes, such as Final Fantasy 3 on DS, without noting that it is a reboot/remake.13. Arcade is a "console". I also do not count PC/Mac/Linux as separate consoles.14. DS/3DS games may include both screens or just one of the screens at my discretion and without warning.15. When answering, you must specify which question you are answering with its number. For Example: Q1: The answer is Super Mario Bros. for NES.16. I am not infallible. If I mess something up, PM me and I'll fix it.INCENTIVE/PRIZES: FREE STUFF!Since I am not a forum official, I cannot offer GPs. There are other incentives though. +3 for rcj8993. Yes to Mi06 and that is Sarge's Heroes, but this screen isn't from N64.+0 for Galdion. So very close.I was able to put together a new Blitz! Though it is rather generic lol. Same rules apply, 2 guesses, per person, per day in addition to the regular questions. Please do peruse the special rules.RULES: 1. Obey the forum's posting rules.2. One game title answer per person per question. If you get something wrong and I correct you, you are allowed to guess again. Once for each time I correct you.3. You can answer both of the non-Blitz! questions in one post. You can answer only two Blitz! questions per day. That makes a total of 4 questions and 8 points possible. If there are Extra Questions posted, you can get even more points by answering as many of those as you'd like.4. Each Blitz! has it's own rules, but I want to reiterate some of the common rules here. Two Blitz! questions, per person, per day. If you are corrected, you may guess again within that day, but only at the two you had initially guessed at. If you pick a question as one of your two that only had the system guess open, it still counts as one of your two questions despite it only being worth 1 point.5. If a question goes unanswered for a certain amount of time or guesses (at my discretion) I will post a new question with the old one in an EXTRA QUESTIONS section.6. Titles are worth one point, a correct system guess is worth 1 bonus point. Extra Questions vary.7. If you change your display name, PM me or include a note in your post along with your next guess and I'll update the scoreboard.8. Games may be repeated.9. As I live in North America, the screens will be of a North American release unless it is unavailable in North America or unless otherwise specified.10. Spelling is at my discretion. If I feel it is obvious that what you typed was intended to be what the correct answer is, points will be rewarded. If I have no idea if it was even in English, then you need to take a typing class.11. I do not include re-releases on WiiWare (Virtual Console), PSN, Steam, etc. as a separate console. For example, Final Fantasy: Mystic Quest was re-released as a download on Wii, but I will not post the Wii version.12. I will do my best to not post full reboots/remakes, such as Final Fantasy 3 on DS, without noting that it is a reboot/remake.13. Arcade is a "console". I also do not count PC/Mac/Linux as separate consoles.14. DS/3DS games may include both screens or just one of the screens at my discretion and without warning.15. When answering, you must specify which question you are answering with its number. For Example: Q1: The answer is Super Mario Bros. for NES.16. I am not infallible. If you get something wrong and I correct you, you are allowed to guess again. Once for each time I correct you.3. You can answer both of the non-Blitz! questions in one post. You can answer only two Blitz! questions per day. That makes a total of 4 questions and 8 points possible. If there are Extra Questions posted, you can get even more points by answering as many of those as you'd like.4. Each Blitz! has it's own rules, but I want to reiterate some of the common rules here. Two Blitz! questions, per person, per day. If you are corrected, you may guess again within that day, but only at the two you had initially guessed at. If you pick a question as one of your two that only had the system guess open, it still counts as one of your two questions despite it only being worth 1 point.5. Titles are worth one point, a correct system guess is worth 1 bonus point. Extra Questions vary.7. If you change your display name, PM me or include a note in your post along with your next guess and I'll update the scoreboard.8. Games may be repeated.9. As I live in North America, the screens will be of a North American release unless it is unavailable in North America or unless otherwise specified.10. Spelling is at my discretion. If I feel it is obvious that what you typed was intended to be what the correct answer is, points will be rewarded. If I have no idea if it was even in English, then you need to take a typing class.11. I do not include re-releases on WiiWare (Virtual Console), PSN, Steam, etc. as a separate console. For example, Final Fantasy: Mystic Quest was re-released as a download on Wii, but I will not post the Wii version.12. I will do my best to not post full reboots/remakes, such as Final Fantasy 3 on DS, without noting that it is a reboot/remake.13. Arcade is a "console". I also do not count PC/Mac/Linux as separate consoles.14. DS/3DS games may include both screens or just one of the screens at my discretion and without warning.15. You can answer only two Blitz! questions per day. That makes a total of 4 questions and 8 points possible. If there are Extra Questions posted, you can get even more points by answering as many of those as you'd like.4. Each Blitz! has it's own rules, but I want to reiterate some of the common rules here. Two Blitz! questions, per person, per day. If you are corrected, you may guess again within that day, but only at the two you had initially guessed at. If you pick a question as one of your two that only had the system guess open, it still counts as one of your two questions despite it only being worth 1 point.5. If you change your display name, PM me or include a note in your post along with your next guess and I'll update the scoreboard.8. Games may be repeated.9. If I feel it is obvious that what you typed was intended to be what the correct answer is, points will be rewarded. If I have no idea if it was even in English, then you need to take a typing class.11. I do not include re-releases on WiiWare (Virtual Console), PSN, Steam, etc. as a separate console. For example, Final Fantasy: Mystic Quest was re-released as a download on Wii, but I will not post the Wii version.12. I will do my best to not post full reboots/remakes, such as Final Fantasy 3 on DS, without noting that it is a reboot/remake.13. Arcade is a "console". I also do not count PC/Mac/Linux as separate consoles.14. DS/3DS games may include both screens or just one of the screens at my discretion and without warning.15. Same rules apply, 2 guesses, per person, per day in addition to the regular questions. Please do peruse the special rules.This Blitz! is all about Pinball. There are 12 questions this time around.RULES: 1. Obey the forum's posting rules.2. One game title answer per person per question. If you get something wrong and I correct you, you are allowed to guess again. Once for each time I correct you.3. You can answer both of the non-Blitz! questions in one post. You can answer only two Blitz! questions per day. That makes a total of 4 questions and 8 points possible. If there are Extra Questions posted, you can get even more points by answering as many of those as you'd like.4. Each Blitz! has it's own rules, but I want to reiterate some of the common rules here. Two Blitz! questions, per person, per day. If you are corrected, you may guess again within that day, but only at the two you had initially guessed at. If you pick a question as one of your two that only had the system guess open, it still counts as one of your two questions despite it only being worth 1 point.5. If a question goes unanswered for a certain amount of time or guesses (at my discretion) I will post a new question with the old one in an EXTRA QUESTIONS section.6. Titles are worth one point, a correct system guess is worth 1 bonus point. Extra Questions vary.7. If you change your display name, PM me or include a note in your post along with your next guess and I'll update the scoreboard.8. Games may be repeated.9. As I live in North America, the screens will be of a North American release unless it is unavailable in North America or unless otherwise specified.10. Spelling is at my discretion. If I feel it is obvious that what you typed was intended to be what the correct answer is, points will be rewarded. If I have no idea if it was even in English, then you need to take a typing class.11. I do not include re-releases on WiiWare (Virtual Console), PSN, Steam, etc. as a separate console. For example, Final Fantasy: Mystic Quest was re-released as a download on Wii, but I will not post the Wii version.12. I will do my best to not post full reboots/remakes, such as Final Fantasy 3 on DS, without noting that it is a reboot/remake.13. Arcade is a "console". I also do not count PC/Mac/Linux as separate consoles.14. DS/3DS games may include both screens or just one of the screens at my discretion and without warning.15. When answering, you must specify which question you are answering with its number. For example: Q1: The answer is Super Mario Bros. for NES.16. I am not infallible. If I mess something up, PM me and I'll fix it.INCENTIVE/PRIZES: FREE STUFF!Since I am not a forum official, I cannot offer GPs. There are other incentives though. New Blitz! Same rules apply, 2 guesses, per person, per day in addition to the regular questions. Please do peruse the special rules.
This Blitz! is all about Zombies of some sort. There are 15 questions this time around.

RULES:
1. Obey the forum's posting rules.
2. One game title answer per person per question. If you get something wrong and I correct you, you are allowed to guess again. Once for each time I correct you.
3. You can answer both of the non-Blitz! questions in one post. You can answer only two Blitz! questions per day. That makes a total of 4 questions and 8 points possible. If there are Extra Questions posted, you can get even more points by answering as many of those as you'd like.
4. Each Blitz! has it's own rules, but I want to reiterate some of the common rules here. Two Blitz! questions, per person, per day. If you are corrected, you may guess again within that day, but only at the two you had initially guessed at. If you pick a question as one of your two that only had the system guess open, it still counts as one of your two questions despite it only being worth 1 point.
5. If a question goes unanswered for a certain amount of time or guesses (at my discretion) I will post a new question with the old one in an EXTRA QUESTIONS section.
6. Titles are worth one point, a correct system guess is worth 1 bonus point. Extra Questions vary.
7. If you change your display name, PM me or include a note in your post along with your next guess and I'll update the scoreboard.
8. Games may be repeated.
9. As I live in North America, the screens will be of a North American release unless it is unavailable in North America or unless otherwise specified.
10. Spelling is at my discretion. If I feel it is obvious that what you typed was intended to be what the correct answer is, points will be rewarded. If I have no idea if it was even in English, then you need to take a typing class.
11. I do not include re-releases on WiiWare (Virtual Console), PSN, Steam, etc. as a separate console. For example, Final Fantasy: Mystic Quest was re-released as a download on Wii, but I will not post the Wii version.
12. I will do my best to not post full reboots/remakes, such as Final Fantasy 3 on DS, without noting that it is a reboot/remake.
13. Arcade is a "console". I also do not count PC/Mac/Linux as separate consoles.
14. DS/3DS games may include both screens or just one of the screens at my discretion and without warning.
15. When answering, you must specify which question you are answering with its number. For example: Q1: The answer is Super Mario Bros. for NES.
16. I am not infallible. If I mess something up, PM me and I'll fix it.

INCENTIVE/PRIZES: FREE STUFF!
Since I am not a forum official, I cannot offer GPs. There are other incentives though. Each post ups your post count and gets you some GPs plus it can be fun to test your gaming knowledge and see your name move up the scoreboard. Here's what I can offer though:
1. Nintendo DS ROMs *
2. Humble Bundle gift urls **
If you get 5 points, PM me and I can send you whichever you like.

New Blitz! Same rules apply, 2 guesses, per person, per day in addition to the regular questions. Please do peruse the special rules.
This Blitz! is all about games based on popular Animes. There are 20 questions this time around.

RULES:
1. Obey the forum's posting rules.
2. One game title answer per person per question. If you get something wrong and I correct you, you are allowed to guess again. Once for each time I correct you.
3. You can answer both of the non-Blitz! questions in one post. You can answer only two Blitz! questions per day. That makes a total of 4 questions and 8 points possible. If there are Extra Questions posted, you can get even more points by answering as many of those as you'd like.
4. Each Blitz! has it's own rules, but I want to reiterate some of the common rules here. Two Blitz! questions, per person, per day. If you are corrected, you may guess again within that day, but only at the two you had initially guessed at. If you pick a question as one of your two that only had the system guess open, it still counts as one of your two questions despite it only being worth 1 point.
5. If a question goes unanswered for a certain amount of time or guesses (at my discretion) I will post a new question with the old one in an EXTRA QUESTIONS section.
6. Titles are worth one point, a correct system guess is worth 1 bonus point. Extra Questions vary.
7. If you change your display name, PM me or include a note in your post along with your next guess and I'll update the scoreboard.
8. Games may be repeated.
9. As I live in North America, the screens will be of a North American release unless it is unavailable in North America or unless otherwise specified.
10. Spelling is at my discretion. If I feel it is obvious that what you typed was intended to be what the correct answer is, points will be rewarded. If I have no idea if it was even in English, then you need to take a typing class.
11. I do not include re-releases on WiiWare (Virtual Console), PSN, Steam, etc. as a separate console. For example, Final Fantasy: Mystic Quest was re-released as a download on Wii, but I will not post the Wii version.
12. I will do my best to not post full reboots/remakes, such as Final Fantasy 3 on DS, without noting that it is a reboot/remake.
13. Arcade is a "console". I also do not count PC/Mac/Linux as separate consoles.
14. DS/3DS games may include both screens or just one of the screens at my discretion and without warning.
15. When answering, you must specify which question you are answering with its number. For example: Q1: The answer is Super Mario Bros. for NES.
16. I am not infallible. If I mess something up, PM me and I'll fix it.

INCENTIVE/PRIZES: FREE STUFF!
Since I am not a forum official, I cannot offer GPs. There are other incentives though. Each post ups your post count and gets you some GPs plus it can be fun to test your gaming knowledge and see your name move up the scoreboard. Here's what I can offer though:
1. Nintendo DS ROMs *
2. Humble Bundle gift urls **
If you get 5 points, PM me and I can send you whichever you like.
* Last I checked I have 280+ NDS ROMs. Here's a link to the text file with a list of all my NDS ROMs. It's updated whenever I grab a new one. Feel free to pick one out every 5 points and PM me. That way, you won't have to spend your DPs and use up the site's bandwidth downloading it.
http://dl.dropbox.co...0Downloaded.txt
** I also have some Humble Bundle gift urls for almost all of the bundles so far. Though I have posted the games for free, the urls will allow you to gain access to keys to get the games on Steam, OnLive, Direct2Drive, and/or Desura. These keys can no longer be acquired as these packs are now closed, so when you get 5 points, feel free to PM me with a request.

New Blitz! Same rules apply, 2 guesses, per person, per day in addition to the regular questions. Please do peruse the special rules.
This Blitz! is all about games based on popular Animes. There are 20 questions this time around.

RULES:
1. Obey the forum's posting rules.
2. One game title answer per person per question. If you get something wrong and I correct you, you are allowed to guess again. Once for each time I correct you.
3. You can answer both of the non-Blitz! questions in one post. You can answer only two Blitz! questions per day. That makes a total of 4 questions and 8 points possible. If there are Extra Questions posted, you can get even more points by answering as many of those as you'd like.
4. Each Blitz! has it's own rules, but I want to reiterate some of the common rules here. Two Blitz! questions, per person, per day. If you are corrected, you may guess again within that day, but only at the two you had initially guessed at. If you pick a question as one of your two that only had the system guess open, it still counts as one of your two questions despite it only being worth 1 point.
5. If a question goes unanswered for a certain amount of time or guesses (at my discretion) I will post a new question with the old one in an EXTRA QUESTIONS section.
6. Titles are worth one point, a correct system guess is worth 1 bonus point. Extra Questions vary.
7. If you change your display name, PM me or include a note in your post along with your next guess and I'll update the scoreboard.
8. Games may be repeated.
9. As I live in North America, the screens will be of a North American release unless it is unavailable in North America or unless otherwise specified.
10. Spelling is at my discretion. If I feel it is obvious that what you typed was intended to be what the correct answer is, points will be rewarded. If I have no idea if it was even in English, then you need to take a typing class.
11. I do not include re-releases on WiiWare (Virtual Console), PSN, Steam, etc. as a separate console. For example, Final Fantasy: Mystic Quest was re-released as a download on Wii, but I will not post the Wii version.
12. I will do my best to not post full reboots/remakes, such as Final Fantasy 3 on DS, without noting that it is a reboot/remake.
13. Arcade is a "console". I also do not count PC/Mac/Linux as separate consoles.
14. DS/3DS games may include both screens or just one of the screens at my discretion and without warning.
15. When answering, you must specify which question you are answering with its number. For example: Q1: The answer is Super Mario Bros. for NES.
16. I am not infallible. If I mess something up, PM me and I'll fix it.

10. Spelling is at my discretion. If I feel it is obvious that what you typed was intended to be what the correct answer is, points will be rewarded. If I have no idea if it was even in English, then you need to take a typing class.
11. I do not include re-releases on WiiWare (Virtual Console), PSN, Steam, etc. as a separate console. For example, Final Fantasy: Mystic Quest was re-released as a download on Wii, but I will not post the Wii version.
12. I will do my best to not post full reboots/remakes, such as Final Fantasy 3 on DS, without noting that it is a reboot/remake.
13. Arcade is a "console". I also do not count PC/Mac/Linux as separate consoles.
14. DS/3DS games may include both screens or just one of the screens at my discretion and without warning.
15. When answering, you must specify which question you are answering with its number. For example: Q1: The answer is Super Mario Bros. for NES.
16. I am not infallible. If I mess something up, PM me and I'll fix it.

13. Arcade is a "console". I also do not count PC/Mac/Linux as separate consoles.
14. DS/3DS games may include both screens or just one of the screens at my discretion and without warning.
15. When answering, you must specify which question you are answering with its number. For example: Q1: The answer is Super Mario Bros. for NES.
16. I am not infallible. If I mess something up, PM me and I'll fix it.

9. As I live in North America, the screens will be of a North American release unless it is unavailable in North America or unless otherwise specified.
10. Spelling is at my discretion. If I feel it is obvious that what you typed was intended to be what the correct answer is, points will be rewarded. If I have no idea if it was even in English, then you need to take a typing class.
11. I do not include re-releases on WiiWare (Virtual Console), PSN, Steam, etc. as a separate console. For example, Final Fantasy: Mystic Quest was re-released as a download on Wii, but I will not post the Wii version.
12. I will do my best to not post full reboots/remakes, such as Final Fantasy 3 on DS, without noting that it is a reboot/remake.
13. Arcade is a "console". I also do not count PC/Mac/Linux as separate consoles.
14. DS/3DS games may include both screens or just one of the screens at my discretion and without warning.
15. When answering, you must specify which question you are answering with its number. For example: Q1: The answer is Super Mario Bros. for NES.
16. I am not infallible. If I mess something up, PM me and I'll fix it.

3. You can answer both of the non-Blitz! questions in one post. You can answer only two Blitz! questions per day. That makes a total of 4 questions and 8 points possible. If there are Extra Questions posted, you can get even more points by answering as many of those as you'd like.
4. Each Blitz! has it's own rules, but I want to reiterate some of the common rules here. Two Blitz! questions, per person, per day. If you are corrected, you may guess again within that day, but only at the two you had initially guessed at. If you pick a question as one of your two that only had the system guess open, it still counts as one of your two questions despite it only being worth 1 point.
5. If a question goes unanswered for a certain amount of time or guesses (at my discretion) I will post a new question with the old one in an EXTRA QUESTIONS section.
6. Titles are worth one point, a correct system guess is worth 1 bonus point. Extra Questions vary.
7. If you change your display name, PM me or include a note in your post along with your next guess and I'll update the scoreboard.
8. Games may be repeated.
9. As I live in North America, the screens will be of a North American release unless it is unavailable in North America or unless otherwise specified.
10. Spelling is at my discretion. If I feel it is obvious that what you typed was intended to be what the correct answer is, points will be rewarded. If I have no idea if it was even in English, then you need to take a typing class.
11. I do not include re-releases on WiiWare (Virtual Console), PSN, Steam, etc. as a separate console. For example, Final Fantasy: Mystic Quest was re-released as a download on Wii, but I will not post the Wii version.
12. I will do my best to not post full reboots/remakes, such as Final Fantasy 3 on DS, without noting that it is a reboot/remake.
13. Arcade is a "console". I also do not count PC/Mac/Linux as separate consoles.
14. DS/3DS games may include both screens or just one of the screens at my discretion and without warning.
15. When answering, you must specify which question you are answering with its number. For example: Q1: The answer is Super Mario Bros. for NES.
16. I am not infallible. If I mess something up, PM me and I'll fix it.

INCENTIVE/PRIZES: FREE STUFF!
Since I am not a forum official, I cannot offer GPs. There are other incentives though. Each post ups your post count and gets you some GPs plus it can be fun to test your gaming knowledge and see your name move up the scoreboard. Here's what I can offer though:
1. Nintendo DS ROMs *
2. Humble Bundle gift urls **
If you get 5 points, PM me and I can send you whichever you like.
* Last I checked I have 280+ NDS ROMs. Here's a link to the text file with a list of all my NDS ROMs. It's updated whenever I grab a new one. Feel free to pick one out every 5 points and PM me. That way, you won't have to spend your DPs and use up the site's bandwidth downloading it.
http://dl.dropbox.co...0Downloaded.txt

These keys can no longer be acquired as these packs are now closed, so when you get 5 points, feel free to PM me with a request.

2. One game title answer per person per question. If you get something wrong and I correct you, you are allowed to guess again. Once for each time I correct you.
3. You can answer both of the non-Blitz! questions in one post. You can answer only two Blitz! questions per day. That makes a total of 4 questions and 8 points possible. If there are Extra Questions posted, you can get even more points by answering as many of those as you'd like.
4. Each Blitz! has it's own rules, but I want to reiterate some of the common rules here. Two Blitz! questions, per person, per day. If you are corrected, you may guess again within that day, but only at the two you had initially guessed at. If you pick a question as one of your two that only had the system guess open, it still counts as one of your two questions despite it only being worth 1 point.
5. If a question goes unanswered for a certain amount of time or guesses (at my discretion) I will post a new question with the old one in an EXTRA QUESTIONS section.
6. Titles are worth one point, a correct system guess is worth 1 bonus point. Extra Questions vary.
7. If you change your display name, PM me or include a note in your post along with your next guess and I'll update the scoreboard.
8. Games may be repeated.
9. As I live in North America, the screens will be of a North American release unless it is unavailable in North America or unless otherwise specified.
10. Spelling is at my discretion. If I feel it is obvious that what you typed was intended to be what the correct answer is, points will be rewarded. If I have no idea if it was even in English, then you need to take a typing class.
11. I do not include re-releases on WiiWare (Virtual Console), PSN, Steam, etc. as a separate console. For example, Final Fantasy: Mystic Quest was re-released as a download on Wii, but I will not post the Wii version.
12. I will do my best to not post full reboots/remakes, such as Final Fantasy 3 on DS, without noting that it is a reboot/remake.
13. Arcade is a "console". I also do not count PC/Mac/Linux as separate consoles.
14. DS/3DS games may include both screens or just one of the screens at my discretion and without warning.
15. When answering, you must specify which question you are answering with its number. For example: Q1: The answer is Super Mario Bros. for NES.
16. I am not infallible. If I mess something up, PM me and I'll fix it.

INCENTIVE/PRIZES: FREE STUFF!
Since I am not a forum official, I cannot offer GPs. There are other incentives though. Each post ups your post count and gets you some GPs plus it can be fun to test your gaming knowledge and see your name move up the scoreboard. Here's what I can offer though:
1. Nintendo DS ROMs *
2. Humble Bundle gift urls **
If you get 5 points, PM me and I can send you whichever you like.
* Last I checked I have 280+ NDS ROMs. Here's a link to the text file with a list of all my NDS ROMs. It's updated whenever I grab a new one. Feel free to pick one out every 5 points and PM me. That way, you won't have to spend your DPs and use up the site's bandwidth downloading it.
http://dl.dropbox.co...0Downloaded.txt
** I also have some Humble Bundle gift urls from a lot of the original byndles. Though I have posted the games for free, the urls will allow you to gain access to keys to get the games on Steam, OnLive, Direct2Drive, and/or Desura. These keys can no longer be acquired as these packs are now closed, so when you get 5 points, feel free to PM me with a request.
